# AtlasBot surface inventory

Date: 2026-11-13. Source baseline: `573bb87530a81c408f613f3cf4273d42fe5465f7`. Scope: Atlas-Bot-Desktop only.

This is a content-search inventory of tracked text files. Each path is assigned once, by its primary area; a file can also contain references from other areas. Internal identifiers, examples, plugin integrations and legal notices are included and are not automatically safe to delete. Binary assets require visual review. No matched key values are included.

Search terms: nightly-labs, copilotkit, openbot, PolyForm, Discord, PostHog, Sentry, Amplitude, Segment, telemetry, analytics, auto-updater and download URL.
